# Third-party licences

Khameleon uses the open-source software, models, fonts and materials below. Generated by `node scripts/generate-licenses.mjs` from what the app actually ships; do not edit by hand. The same list is served inside the app (About Khameleon → Licences → "All third-party licences").

**349 software packages** (285 MIT, 29 ISC, 12 Apache-2.0, 6 BSD-2-Clause, 5 BlueOak-1.0.0, 3 BSD-3-Clause, 2 OFL-1.1, 2 Unlicense, 1 BSD, 1 (MIT OR GPL-3.0-or-later), 1 (MIT AND Zlib), 1 (BSD-2-Clause OR MIT OR Apache-2.0), 1 0BSD) **and 6 other items.** Every licence allows commercial use; the generator refuses any that doesn't.

Not used, by decision: Anthropic's Word/PDF/PowerPoint/Excel skills (restricted licence), Anthropic's doc-coauthoring skill (no licence), Llama models (licence conditions), Qwen2.5-3B/72B (non-Apache licences), Ollama (not needed).

## Models, fonts and other items

### Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (GGUF)

Khami's built-in language brain (downloaded at setup from huggingface.co/Qwen). Licence: **Apache-2.0**. Copyright 2024 Alibaba Cloud.

```
Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (see the Apache 2.0 text under any Apache-2.0 package below). Model cards: https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-GGUF and https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF. Khameleon does not use the Qwen name or branding in the product.
```

### llama.cpp (ggml)

Runs Khami on the user's computer. Licence: **MIT**. Copyright (c) 2023-2026 The ggml authors.

```
MIT licence - standard text under "Standard licence texts" at the end, with copyright (c) 2023-2026 The ggml authors.
```

### Tesseract English language data (eng.traineddata)

On-device OCR of scanned documents (downloaded once on first use). Licence: **Apache-2.0**. Copyright Google Inc. and Tesseract contributors.

```
Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0. Source: https://github.com/tesseract-ocr/tessdata_fast
```

### Electron and Chromium

Desktop app. Licence: **MIT**. Copyright (c) Electron contributors; Chromium: The Chromium Authors and others.

```
Electron is MIT-licensed (text under the electron package below). Chromium and its components carry their own notices, shipped inside the installed app as LICENSES.chromium.html next to the Electron licence.
```

### JetBrains Mono, Space Grotesk, Inter, Plus Jakarta Sans (fonts)

Typefaces in the app (shipped with it - nothing is loaded from Google Fonts). Licence: **OFL-1.1**. JetBrains s.r.o.; Florian Karsten; The Inter Project Authors; The Plus Jakarta Sans Project Authors.

```
SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1 - https://openfontlicense.org. The fonts may be used, bundled and distributed freely with software; they may not be sold on their own.
```

### Anthropic "internal-comms" and "skill-creator" skills

Adapted into Khameleon's "Internal communications" and "Skill writer" skills. Licence: **Apache-2.0**. Copyright 2025 Anthropic, PBC.

```
Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0. MODIFIED: rewritten and adapted for Khameleon (Skill writer: instructions only).
```
